je_tyrone_cp_famine_abruzzo = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/quinine.dds"
	group = je_group_historical_content
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_ABRUZZO = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/quinine.dds"
	group = je_group_historical_content
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_AWADH = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {

			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/quinine.dds"
	group = je_group_historical_content
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_CENTRAL_CUBA = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no

		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}

		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}

	group = je_group_historical_content
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_MERZ = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak

	group = je_group_historical_content
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_ORLEANS = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}

			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_SARGASSO_SEA = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_WESTERN_CARIBBEAN_SEA = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_WEST_VIRGINIA = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}

		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}
je_tyrone_cp_famine_zulia = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/quinine.dds"
	group = je_group_historical_content
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = cp_india
		s:STATE_ZULIA = {
			save_scope_as = cp_famine_region
		}
	}
	complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			NOT = {
				has_harvest_condition = disease_outbreak
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
		scope:cp_famine_region = {
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_active
			remove_variable = tyrone_cp_famine_end
			set_variable = {
				name = tyrone_cp_famine_cooldown
				days = tyrone_cp_three_years
			}
		}
	}
	invalid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = tyrone_cp_forced_active
		}
	}
	progressbar = no
	weight = 20
}
